# IT & Support and Security & Devices Completion Goal

**Source design:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-18-it-support-native-monitoring-platform-design.md`

**Branch:** `codex/it-security-monitoring-design`

**Status:** Implementation in progress

> **Mandatory architecture boundary:** Oblivion Findings is one application for one operating organisation across all configured sites. New work must not add customer-partition selectors, partitions, contracts, or acceptance fixtures. Use roles and permissions, approved sites and networks, canonical ownership, direct-object denial, and privacy rules. See the application architecture standard.

> **Mandatory product-surface boundary:** IT & Support, Security & Devices, Fleet and native Monitoring are desktop/web application experiences. Do not add mobile-specific pages, navigation, acceptance scope or a separate mobile store. Any retained legacy mobile URL may only redirect through the same permission boundary to its canonical desktop workspace.

## Completion rule

This ledger is the master acceptance record. The goal is complete only when every stream is `Acceptance verified`, its evidence links are recorded, the full regression/build gates pass, and the user has not left a material gap unaccepted. A merged or pushed branch is not the same as acceptance verification. Production deployment is separately authorised.

## Existing capability retained

The current IT foundation already includes requester self-service, agent queues, ticket references, comments/internal notes, attachments, watchers, merge, approval, CSAT, SLA policies and business hours, reports, knowledge, inbound email ingestion, mailbox connections, and onboarding-linked provisioning. These capabilities are migration inputs and regression obligations, not work to recreate under new names.

Security & Devices already provides the canonical `Device`, assignments, topology relationships, groups, events, maintenance, documents, asset links, provider integrations, and a DeviceEvent-to-Control Room signal bridge. Control Room remains the only operational signal/alert correlation engine.

## Requirement matrix

### Architecture and monitoring

- [x] A01 Laravel/Inertia control plane and monitoring-runtime boundary are implemented.
- [ ] A02 Central SD-WAN monitoring works without a collector.
- [ ] A03 Optional remote collector is securely enrolled, scoped, buffered, monitored, revoked, and recovered.
- [x] A04 Durable versioned observation/event/command contracts are idempotent and replayable.
- [ ] A05 Time-series storage and retention tiers are implemented with business-record references in MySQL.
- [x] A06 Discovery scopes, runs, candidates, matching, merge/split, and exclusions are complete.
- [ ] A07 ICMP, TCP, DNS, HTTP/HTTPS, TLS, SNMPv3, traps, syslog, flow, SSH/WinRM-approved checks, and provider APIs are covered.
- [ ] A08 Monitor profiles, coverage gaps, dependencies, maintenance, confirmation, hysteresis, stale/unknown, baselines, and roll-ups are complete.
- [x] A09 Topology snapshots, evidence/confidence, changes, maps, and root-cause suppression are complete.
- [ ] A10 Configuration/inventory snapshots, diff, firmware, and capacity history are complete.

- 2026-08-11: V09 protected load/soak evidence advanced by closing an unbounded release-authority replay window and repairing the connected non-release publication regression without claiming a deployed load exercise. Unlike the adjacent monitoring authorities, the exact source/attestation-bound load/soak authority accepted an arbitrarily long validity interval, allowing the same old run to remain release-authorised for months or years. Its validity window must now be positive and no longer than 24 hours; the existing exactly-24-hour fixture remains valid and a one-second-over adversary fails. Focused execution also exposed that the recent output re-open check unconditionally demanded Linux release-mode owner/`0600` semantics even for explicit test-authority and intentionally unverified artifacts, although their writer deliberately applies those permission requirements only to genuine release output. Exact-byte and stable-identity re-open checks now remain unconditional, while private owner/mode checks remain conditional on real release provenance as the runbook states. The signed exercise already bound replacement issue/consumption, five-sample replacement transport, replacement and general Site token-denial records, the replacement heartbeat and restored service, but either denial could be timestamped before replacement transport completed or after the heartbeat while the overall exercise still passed. Both token-denial timestamps must now be at or after replacement transport completion and at or before the replacement heartbeat; the two denial attempts may occur in either order. Focused adversaries prove an early replacement-token reuse denial and a late general-token denial both fail. The complete collector release-evidence Unit suite passed 24 tests / 69 assertions and the collector Architecture boundary passed 4 / 165; PHP syntax, scoped Pint and diff hygiene passed. - 2026-08-11: A07/A08 independently signed protocol-policy evidence advanced by closing two evidence-class substitution false-successes without running or claiming the real targets/providers/policy drills. The signed matrix required all 14 protocol/provider rows, nine policy rows and six ordered transition drills, but its four top-level supervised-process/provider-audit/target-side-log/operator-signoff references were only shape-checked and could all reuse one hash. A protocol row could likewise use its runtime observation hash as its target-side execution proof. A scheduler-direct, value-free external heartbeat is now implemented and withheld unless all eight workers plus the SNMP-trap, syslog and flow listeners are current; exact-host public-HTTPS egress is DNS-pinned, rejects private/reserved addresses and query credentials, persists no URL or response body, and exposes only bounded operator state. - L06 complete locally: finite retry, immutable idempotency, ordered delivery, exact-byte replay, sequence gaps, poison-message dead letters, clock drift, backlog age, durable recovery intents, audited operator takeover, discard-without-delete, runtime/queue health and permission-scoped drill-down are visible and recoverable. Live remote-Site observation remains A03/V10 deployment evidence rather than an unimplemented recovery workflow.
- A05 and A10 implementation partial: governed external time-series pointers/current summaries, raw-hourly-daily retention tiers, privacy/legal-hold deletion, value-free tombstones, capacity projections, and encrypted configuration/inventory snapshots with bounded value-free diffs now exist. Signed flow buckets, safe numeric monitor observations, provider snapshots, and approved SSH/WinRM inventory use these stores and preserve the canonical Device. Automated pointer/hash reconciliation, load evidence, desktop operations proof, and a fail-closed read-only restored-runtime verifier now cover relational continuity, delivery/projection gaps, provider cursors, Redis, InfluxDB and the snapshot store. Real storage endpoints, production retention execution, and an isolated rehearsal against restored MySQL, Redis, InfluxDB, object storage and the secret manager remain release gates, so neither requirement is overstated as acceptance complete.
- V09 partial: executable load, soak, latency, collector outage/return, central recovery, provider rate-limit, uncertain-result, reconciliation-backlog, credential-compromise and runbook evidence is recorded. The restored-runtime verifier now fails non-zero on relational reconciliation gaps or unavailable Redis, time-series and snapshot dependencies without writing to them or disclosing endpoints, credentials or provider cursors. The source design still requires an isolated real MySQL/stream/time-series/object-store/secret-manager restore rehearsal, so V09 remains open until that evidence is captured.

Progress note: M07 is complete locally. Client personal-safety and staff operational-safety tracking use assignment-specific purpose, authority, audience, collection and retention controls; exact active consent is required where applicable. Expiry, withdrawal and assignment end stop collection, clear current-location projections, deny API/history/export/direct links, redact reusable location metadata, and remove cached UI state on focus/visibility recheck. Governed export requires permission plus an audited operational reason, and assignment-specific physical retention prunes location evidence without erasing permitted operational totals. Generic Fleet device detail never exposes personal-location telemetry and directs authorised users to the governed Tracking or Client surface.

Progress note: M03 is complete locally. The provider-neutral catalogue now defines 35 bounded actions across doors and credentials, alarms, cameras, remote sessions and shell, reboot, wipe, configuration, firmware, network policy, healthcare control, facilities, and device/service/Site suppression. Every high-risk action requires fresh step-up, independent approval or an eligible IT Change, a fresh observation, short expiry, explicit impact acknowledgement, signed evidence, immutable audit, and reconcile-before-retry. Critical actions additionally require configured MFA, exact Device-name confirmation, and no more than 120 seconds before expiry; destructive, life-safety, broad Site suppression, and healthcare controls do not permit break glass where policy forbids it. Ordinary suppression cannot accept a Site scope, and a policy change before dispatch closes the request safely without an adapter call. The focused registry passed 6 tests / 740 assertions, the connected request/approval/dispatch/break-glass/provider/Profile suite passed 60 / 587, and the Management UI passed 8 tests. TypeScript, scoped ESLint/Prettier/Pint, PHP syntax, and the 5,002-module client / 1,654-module SSR builds passed. Exact-worktree Dusk passed 2 / 154 at 1440 x 900 and 1280 x 800 for normal approval and governed break glass, including impact guidance, acknowledgement, safe queue handoff, evidence, and no horizontal overflow. Unsupported provider actions remain honestly unavailable; collector-backed command delivery, governed bulk management, live provider/secret-manager proof, commit, push, and deployment remain open.

Progress note: M01 is complete locally. Every one of the 35 provider-neutral management capabilities now declares allowed workspace domains, Device classes, source permissions, sensitivity and required management level. One shared authorization service applies the ordered Observe, Operate, Manage, Control and Admin lattice while preserving explicit-deny precedence, then revalidates approved application access, canonical Site/ownership visibility, workspace, Device class, source-domain permission, personal-tracking purpose/authority/consent/audience, and sensitive CCTV/healthcare boundaries across presentation, request, approval, dispatch and break-glass review. Inaccessible context is concealed without leaking actions or history; policy drift at approval or dispatch fails closed and performs no adapter call. The connected management matrix passed 63 tests / 1,426 assertions and the Management UI passed 9 tests; TypeScript, scoped ESLint/Prettier/Pint, PHP syntax, diff checks, and the 5,002-module client / 1,654-module SSR production builds passed. Exact-worktree Dusk passed 1 / 14 at 1440 x 900 and 1280 x 800, proving non-revealing CCTV action concealment, permission-based reveal, high-risk and unavailable-adapter guidance, and no horizontal overflow. The temporary production-asset server was stopped with no listener or PHP server left on port 8765. No live command, production credential, commit, push, or deployment was used. At the M01 checkpoint, M05, collector-backed runtime delivery, live provider/secret-manager proof, and master release gates remained open.

Progress note: M05 is complete locally. Secret-manager references remain encrypted and hidden while every broker-issued one-use lease is registered against the exact reference version, Site and capabilities. Central workers receive bounded material transiently; enrolled collectors receive only an identity-bound sealed box whose signed outer and inner scopes must agree, and both paths erase material after use. Rotation or revocation contains all outstanding grants, retries provider revocation without retaining plaintext, and authoritatively erases the encrypted lease identifier at expiry; a one-minute maintenance job reconciles the registry. Application logging channels now recursively redact credentials, authorisation headers, URL user-info, sensitive assignments and private keys, exception payloads are reduced to safe evidence, validation does not flash secret-reference fields, and sensitive parameters are annotated. The executable compromise runbook and verifier prove old-version containment plus replacement rotation/retest without revealing identifiers. Focused backend, unit, architecture, collector and frontend verification passed; TypeScript, scoped ESLint/Prettier/Pint, PHP syntax, diff checks, and the 5,002-module client / 1,654-module SSR production builds passed. Exact-worktree Dusk passed 1 / 19 at 1440 x 900 and 1280 x 800, proving safe alias, lease/revocation counts, rotation health, no external reference or lease identifier disclosure, no severe console errors and no horizontal overflow. The production-asset server was stopped, port 8765 is closed, and `public/hot` is absent. No live secret-manager credential, provider command, commit, push or deployment was used; live-provider and master release evidence remain separate open gates.

## Evidence log

- 2026-08-11: A05 production-retention evidence advanced by closing a post-publication endpoint/release-identity race and output-directory ownership gap without executing retention. The command already re-probed endpoints and re-read its clean checkout and protected authority immediately before writing, but those checks sat outside the writer's publication cleanup boundary. Endpoint, revision or authority change during the JSON/checksum writes could therefore leave an accepted pair, and the writer allowed any writable directory with no group/other bits without requiring exact mode `0700`, service-account ownership or stable directory identity. The writer now accepts a final pre-commit callback after both flushed/synced writes but before success; the command uses it to re-probe the live MySQL/Influx identities, revalidate the signed endpoint attestation, recheck the clean exact checkout and re-read the protected authority. Any failure removes only the newly created pair. The writer also requires exact service-account-owned mode `0700`, publishes exact mode-`0600` files, verifies complete stable file identity and rechecks the directory identity before commit. New behavior proves cleanup after an intentional final-identity failure and refuses non-exact directory mode. Focused Unit and Architecture proof passed 11 tests / 89 assertions; the MySQL-backed local-runtime refusal passed 1 / 4; PHP syntax, scoped Pint and diff hygiene passed. A05 remains open for the independently approved real MySQL/Influx retention run against representative roll-up, privacy and legal-hold cohorts, with immutable retained evidence. The master remains exactly 59 of 69 complete (85.5%), with 10 requirements / 14.5% remaining. No live credential, endpoint, retention mutation, authority record, evidence artifact, deployment, commit, push or external message was used.

The audit proved that governed queue configuration already matched work type, category, priority, affected service and Site, and transactional intake already accepted agent-supplied work type/service values, but the main `Log & triage ticket` journey never captured either field. - 2026-08-03: V10 truthfulness advanced by completing the previously backend-only Device Group automatic-membership journey. Operators can now author and edit an allowlisted all/any rule with up to eight plain-language conditions, preview unsaved matches without mutation, and read the saved rule on the group page. Strict nested validation rejects unknown fields/operators, extra keys, blank or oversized scalar values, empty/duplicate/oversized lists and malformed rules rather than silently storing ignored input; malformed historic JSON also fails closed, preserves existing membership and directs the operator to repair it. Applying membership is separated from saving: a governed review dialog first shows exact added, removed, kept and final counts plus a bounded visible sample, and only its explicit apply action mutates the visible Site-scoped membership. The initial backend contracts exposed the missing edit payload and draft-preview route; the initial frontend contract failed because the builder did not exist.
